### Resume Graph Execution from Checkpoint

Source: https://github.com/langflow-ai/langflow/blob/v1.12.0/docs/features/durable-execution-hitl.md

Initializes a graph from a stored checkpoint and records the human-in-the-loop decision event span.

```python
graph = LfxGraph.resume_from_checkpoint(checkpoint, checkpoint_store=store)
graph.flow_name = graph.flow_name or flow_name
await graph.initialize_run()                      # re-init tracing on the original run_id
decision = reroute_decision_on_timeout(pending, resume["decision"])
graph.human_input_decisions = {resume["request_id"]: decision}
action_id = str((decision or {}).get("action_id", ""))
# HITL actions are user-defined (Approve/Reject/Remove/...), so label the span with the chosen
# action's button label (from the pending options), not a hardcoded approve/reject binary.
gate_label = _hitl_gate_label(action_id, (pending or {}).get("options"))
graph.tracing_service.record_event_span(
    span_id=f"hitl-{resume['request_id']}",
    name=f"Human In The Loop — {gate_label}",
    outputs={"decision": action_id},
)
# restore built vertices; re-run only non-input predecessors of the gated vertex
```

### Behavior Specifications > Feature: A flow pauses for a human decision and resumes durably

Source: https://github.com/langflow-ai/langflow/blob/v1.12.0/docs/features/durable-execution-hitl.md

When a flow reaches a gated tool or HumanInput node, the graph checkpoints and the job status becomes SUSPENDED. This mechanism ensures that the flow can survive server restarts, as the graph is rebuilt from the checkpoint upon resumption rather than being re-executed from the beginning.

--------------------------------

### Durable Execution and Human-in-the-Loop > Domain Model > Domain Events

Source: https://github.com/langflow-ai/langflow/blob/v1.12.0/docs/features/durable-execution-hitl.md

The system relies on domain events to coordinate between components. When a component triggers a pause, a human input request is generated, leading to a suspended job status. Once a valid decision is claimed, the system resumes by rebuilding the graph and initializing tracing, eventually marking the job as completed upon final execution.

--------------------------------

### Overview > Summary

Source: https://github.com/langflow-ai/langflow/blob/v1.12.0/docs/features/durable-execution-hitl.md

Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) allows flows to pause mid-execution to request human approval, rejection, or input. This pause is durable, meaning it is checkpointed to the database and can survive process restarts without requiring a re-run of completed work. The entire process is observable as a single backend trace, capturing the approval step and terminal output as distinct spans.

--------------------------------

### Behavior Specifications > Scenario: Single-flight resume

Source: https://github.com/langflow-ai/langflow/blob/v1.12.0/docs/features/durable-execution-hitl.md

The system enforces single-flight resume logic, ensuring that if multiple resume requests arrive simultaneously for the same job, only one succeeds. Additionally, if a decision arrives after a defined timeout policy, the system uses deterministic rerouting to resolve the decision.
